=== UNIVERSAL THOUSAND SONS RULE TEXT TARGETS ===
RULE id=r18-ts-praetor-psyker-rule name=Sorcerers of Prospero — Praetor host=hq-praetor hidden=true
In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Praetor is a Psyker (Mastery Level 2) and selects two powers from Biomancy, Divination, Pyromancy, Telekinesis or Telepathy. Activating a Force Weapon counts as the use of a psychic power, and the same power may not be used more than once in the same player turn.
RULE id=r18-ts-centurion-psyker-rule name=Sorcerers of Prospero — Independent Character host=hq-centurion hidden=true
In a Thousand Sons Detachment, a Legion Centurion and its Consul variants are Psykers (Mastery Level 1) unless another rule grants a higher Mastery Level. A Librarian Consul follows the normal Librarian upgrade rules; an Epistolary is Mastery Level 2.
RULE id=r25-legion-xv-reference name=Thousand Sons — Legion Rules & Armoury host=NONE hidden=false
LEGION SPECIAL RULES SORCERERS OF PROSPERO The Thousand Sons possess an unparalleled concentration of psychic talent amongst the Legiones Astartes, and the study of the Warp permeates every level of the Legion. All Thousand Sons Independent Characters are Psykers. Unless they already possess a higher Psychic Mastery Level, they count as Psyker (Mastery Level 1). A Thousand Sons Praetor instead counts as Psyker (Mastery Level 2). Thousand Sons Psykers may select their psychic powers from the following ProHammer Psychic Disciplines: • Biomancy • Divination • Pyromancy • Telekinesis • Telepathy The number of powers known and which may be invoked is determined by the Psyker's Mastery Level and the normal ProHammer psychic rules. A Thousand Sons Librarian Consul follows these rules normally. Activating a Force Weapon counts as the use of a psychic power. A Psyker may not use the same psychic power more than once during the same player turn. THE PROSPERINE CULTS The warriors of the Thousand Sons were organised according to the great psychic Cults of Prospero, each devoted to the mastery of a particular aspect of the psychic arts. Every Thousand Sons Infantry unit and Independent Character must be assigned to one of the following Prosperine Cults when the army is selected: • Pavoni • Raptora • Corvidae • Athanaeans • Pyrae The chosen Cult must be recorded on the army roster. Every unit receives the normal Cult Arcana of its chosen Cult. A model or unit may normally belong to only one Prosperine Cult. An Independent Character retains his own Cult when joining a unit belonging to another Cult. Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery are not conferred between the Independent Character and the unit unless specifically stated otherwise. CULT MASTERY A unit with the Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers special rule has fully manifested the teachings of its Prosperine Cult and additionally receives the Cult Mastery benefit listed for that Cult. Cult Mastery does not require a Psychic Test and does not count as the use of a psychic power. PAVONI — QUICKBLOOD The Pavoni practise mastery over the biological processes of the body, accelerating muscle, nerve and reflex far beyond even the already superhuman limits of the Legiones Astartes. CULT ARCANA Pavoni units gain the Fleet special rule. CULT MASTERY A Pavoni Psyker or Psychic Brotherhood also gains the Crusader special rule. RAPTORA — KINE SHIELDS The Raptora manipulate telekinetic force, surrounding themselves with barriers capable of deflecting incoming fire. CULT ARCANA Models in a Raptora unit gain a 6+ Invulnerable Save against shooting attacks. This does not improve an existing Invulnerable Save. CULT MASTERY Models in a Raptora Psyker unit or Psychic Brotherhood instead gain a 5+ Invulnerable Save against shooting attacks. If a model already possesses an Invulnerable Save, improve that save by 1 against shooting attacks instead, to a maximum of 4+. In addition, the unit improves any Cover Save it receives against shooting attacks by 1, to a maximum of 3+. These benefits have no effect in close combat. CORVIDAE — PRECOGNITIVE STRIKE The Corvidae glimpse fragments of possible futures, allowing them to anticipate the actions of their enemies before they occur. CULT ARCANA When a Corvidae unit resolves a First Fire shooting attack, it may re-roll To Hit rolls of 1. This bonus applies to all ranged weapons fired by the unit as part of that First Fire attack. CULT MASTERY A Corvidae Psyker unit or Psychic Brotherhood may also re-roll To Hit rolls of 1 when making: • Overwatch attacks • Return Fire attacks • Stand & Shoot attacks ATHANAEANS — DISCIPLINE OF THE MIND The Athanaeans possess extraordinary mental discipline and specialise in telepathy, psychic communication and the defence of the mind against outside influence. CULT ARCANA Athanaean units gain: • Stubborn • Re-roll failed Pinning tests CULT MASTERY An Athanaean Psyker unit or Psychic Brotherhood additionally gains the Adamantium Will special rule. PYRAE — ASHEN BLOW The Pyrae manipulate heat and psychic flame, surrounding themselves with destructive energies as they close upon the enemy. CULT ARCANA Pyrae units gain the Hammer of Wrath special rule. CULT MASTERY Close-combat attacks and Flame weapons used by models in a Pyrae Psyker unit or Psychic Brotherhood gain the Soul Blaze special rule. PSYCHIC BROTHERHOODS The Thousand Sons routinely gathered their most psychically gifted veterans into circles capable of focusing their abilities through collective discipline. The following units may purchase the Brotherhood of Psykers special rule for +25 points per squad: • Legion Veteran Squad • Legion Terminator Squad A unit upgraded in this manner counts as a Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1). The unit then selects one psychic power from the ProHammer Psychic Discipline associated with its chosen Prosperine Cult, as shown below: Discipline Pavoni Biomancy RaptoraTelekinesis Corvidae Divination Athanaeans Telepathy Pyrae Pyromancy The unit follows all normal ProHammer rules for Brotherhood of Psykers. The unit additionally gains the Cult Mastery benefit of its chosen Prosperine Cult. An Independent Character which joins a Psychic Brotherhood remains a separate Psyker and does not combine its Mastery Level, psychic powers or Psychic Tests with those of the Brotherhood. PRICE OF KNOWLEDGE The Thousand Sons maintained an unusually large body of officers, scholars and specialist formations, but the concentration of such irreplaceable warriors carried its own risks. A Thousand Sons Detachment may include: • one additional HQ selection; • one additional Elites selection; • one fewer Fast Attack selection. When using the Standard Force Organisation Chart, this gives: HQ: 1–3 Elites: 0–4 Fast Attack: 0–2 The Thousand Sons player must identify on the army roster which HQ and Elites selections occupy the additional slots. If Victory Points are being used, each unit selected using an additional HQ or Elites selection awards the opposing player an additional 50 Victory Points if completely destroyed. SIGNS AND PORTENTS The psychic communion which gives the Thousand Sons their extraordinary strength also leaves the Legion vulnerable to psychic catastrophe. The Flesh Change is never entirely absent from the thoughts of Magnus' sons. Whenever a Thousand Sons Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers suffers one or more Wounds as a result of Perils of the Warp, every friendly non-vehicle unit with the Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) special rule must immediately take a Pinning test In addition, if every Thousand Sons Independent Character in the Detachment has been slain, all surviving units with the Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) special rule: • suffer -1 Leadership for the remainder of the battle; • may no longer make Pursuit moves. THOUSAND SONS ARMOURY The armouries of the XV Legion combine conventional Legiones Astartes technology with psycho-reactive materials, occult devices and weapons whose operation relies upon the psychic disciplines of Prospero. PROSPERINE FORCE WEAPON — POWER WEAPON +10 POINTS Any Thousand Sons model permitted to select a Power Weapon may upgrade that Power Weapon to a Force Weapon for +10 points. A Prosperine Force Weapon follows the normal rules for Force Weapons. If a model belonging to a Psychic Brotherhood inflicts one or more unsaved wounds with a Force Weapon, nominate one model in that Brotherhood which inflicted such a wound. The Brotherhood may make a single Psychic Test to activate that model's Force Weapon. If passed, only the unsaved wounds inflicted by that nominated model's Force Weapon become Massive Wounds and inflict D3 Wounds instead. A Psychic Brotherhood may attempt only one Force Weapon activation during each Assault phase, regardless of how many models in the unit carry Force Weapons. ARCANE LITANIES — 10 POINTS Any Thousand Sons Independent Character with Psychic Mastery Level 1 or higher may purchase Arcane Litanies for +10 points. Once per battle, when the bearer suffers a Wound from Perils of the Warp, it may ignore that Wound. The Psychic Test and psychic power are otherwise resolved normally. ÆTHER-FIRE CANNON — FREE UPGRADE Any Thousand Sons model equipped with a Plasma Cannon may upgrade it to an Æther-fire Cannon at no additional points cost. If a unit contains more than one Plasma Cannon, either all or none of them must be upgraded. Weapon | Range | Strength | AP | Special Rules Æther-fire Cannon | 36" | 7 | 2 | Heavy 1, Blast, Gets Hot, Soul Blaze ASPHYX SHELLS A Thousand Sons Independent Character may purchase Asphyx Shells for +10 points. A Legion Veteran Squad or Legion Terminator Squad may purchase Asphyx Shells for +20 points per squad. Asphyx Shells may be used with Bolt Pistols, Bolters, Combi-Bolters and the Bolter component of Combi-Weapons. Weapons firing Asphyx Shells gain the Shred special rule. Asphyx Shells may not be combined with Special Issue Ammunition or another ammunition upgrade. TELEPORTATION TRANSPONDERS Any Thousand Sons unit composed entirely of models wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +15 points per unit. A Thousand Sons Independent Character wearing any form of Terminator Armour may purchase them for +10 points. A model or unit equipped with Teleportation Transponders gains the Deep Strike special rule and may deploy using Deep Strike even if the mission would not normally permit it. An Independent Character intending to Deep Strike as part of another unit must purchase Teleportation Transponders separately. PROSPERINE ÆTHER-DISC — 30 POINTS Any Thousand Sons Independent Character wearing Power Armour or Artificer Armour may purchase a Prosperine Æther-Disc for +30 points. The bearer becomes Jump Infantry and follows all normal ProHammer rules for Jump Infantry. In addition, the bearer gains the Turbo-Boosters and Hammer of Wrath special rules. ÆTHERIC EVASION If the bearer moved at least 6" during its preceding Movement phase, enemy models suffer -1 on their To Hit rolls when attacking the bearer in close combat, to a maximum required roll of 6+. This benefit is lost while the bearer is Falling Back. A Prosperine Æther-Disc may not be combined with a Jump Pack, Bike, Jetbike or any form of Terminator Armour. THOUSAND SONS RITE OF WAR: THE AXIS OF DISSOLUTION The Axis of Dissolution is a carefully prepared tactical configuration in which the enemy is drawn against positions selected in advance by the Legion's seers, held in place by disciplined resistance and destroyed as the pattern of battle unfolds. EFFECTS THE ALEMBIC OF ADAMANT Thousand Sons units automatically pass Morale and Pinning tests while they have at least one model within 6" of an Objective. If the mission does not use Objective markers, after deployment zones have been determined the Thousand Sons player may nominate one terrain feature outside the enemy deployment zone. The centre of that terrain feature counts as an Objective for the purposes of this rule only. THE CAUSTIC OF GRACE Thousand Sons Infantry units may enter Overwatch even if they moved during their preceding Movement phase, provided they did not Advance. All other ProHammer requirements for entering Overwatch apply normally. THE TRANSITION OF VITRIOL Thousand Sons models may re-roll failed To Hit and To Wound rolls when attacking an enemy unit which is Falling Back. Against Vehicles affected by a mission-specific retreat or withdrawal rule, re-roll failed Armour Penetration rolls instead of failed To Wound rolls. LIMITATIONS Every Troops choice in the Detachment must be taken at its maximum permitted unit size. The Detachment may not contain more Vehicles with the Tank or Flyer type than it contains Infantry units. The Detachment may not include a Fortification. THOUSAND SONS RITE OF WAR: THE GUARD OF THE CRIMSON KING When the most powerful formations of the XV Legion were committed to battle, the Sekhmet and the Legion's greatest sorcerers could be concentrated into a single overwhelming psychic spearhead. EFFECTS ASTRAL WARFARE The first two psychic powers successfully invoked by Thousand Sons Psykers during each player turn are ignored when determining the penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp. All other ProHammer psychic rules apply normally. WREATHED IN LIGHTNING, THEY REND THE VEIL All Thousand Sons units composed entirely of models wearing Terminator Armour gain Teleportation Transponders at no additional points cost. Thousand Sons Independent Characters may purchase Teleportation Transponders for +10 points regardless of the armour they wear. Whenever a unit or Character using these Transponders arrives by Deep Strike, it gains the Fear special rule and may re-roll Invulnerable Save rolls of 1 until the beginning of the next Thousand Sons player turn. INITIATES OF THE SCARAB Sekhmet Terminator Cabals may be selected as Troops choices and must fulfil the Detachment's compulsory Troops selections. THE BIDDING OF THE CRIMSON KING Magnus the Red may fulfil a compulsory HQ selection when using this Rite and does not occupy a Lord of War selection. LIMITATIONS The army's Warlord must be Magnus the Red, Ahzek Ahriman or a Thousand Sons Praetor. A Thousand Sons Praetor selected as the Warlord may be upgraded from Mastery Level 2 to Mastery Level 3 for +25 points. The Detachment may not contain more Vehicle units than units with the Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) special rule. The Detachment may not include an Allied Detachment or Fortification. THOUSAND SONS RITE OF WAR: THE FELLOWSHIPS OF PROSPERO The Thousand Sons were organised into great Fellowships whose warriors trained and fought beneath circles of powerful psykers. In some formations entire battle companies acted as extensions of their sorcerous commanders, combining Legion discipline with the collective psychic gifts of Prospero. EFFECTS CIRCLES OF INITIATES A Legion Tactical Squad containing 20 models may purchase Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1\) for +25 points per squad. A Tactical Squad upgraded in this manner selects one psychic power from the normal Psychic Disciplines available to Thousand Sons Psykers and gains the Cult Mastery benefit of its chosen Prosperine Cult. The unit follows all normal ProHammer rules for Brotherhood of Psykers. FELLOWSHIP VETERANS Legion Veteran Squads and Legion Terminator Squads purchase Brotherhood of Psykers for +15 points per squad instead of the normal +25 points. MAGISTER TEMPLI At the beginning of each Thousand Sons player turn, the Warlord may nominate one friendly Psychic Brotherhood within 12". Until the beginning of the next Thousand Sons player turn, that Brotherhood may use the Warlord's Leadership when taking Psychic Tests. This specifically overrides the normal rule preventing a Psychic Brotherhood from using the Leadership of an attached Independent Character. ORDER OF THE CULTS Every Psychic Brotherhood in the Detachment must belong to the same Prosperine Cult as at least one Thousand Sons Independent Character in the army. LIMITATIONS The army's Warlord must be a Thousand Sons Psyker. The Detachment must include at least two Psychic Brotherhoods. A Legion Tactical Squad may only purchase Brotherhood of Psykers if it contains 20 models. The Detachment may include no more than one Fast Attack choice. The Detachment may not include an Allied Detachment.
RULE id=r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-source name=Source Entry host=r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king hidden=true
Primarch of the Thousand Sons, the Crimson King, Lord of Prospero Magnus the Red was the most psychically gifted of the Emperor’s sons, a towering scholar-warrior whose understanding of the immaterium exceeded that of all but the greatest minds of the Imperium. Upon Prospero he gathered philosophers, occultists and psykers into a civilisation devoted to knowledge, while the Thousand Sons themselves became masters of disciplines that other Legions regarded with suspicion and fear. Upon the battlefield Magnus wielded sorcery as naturally as another warrior might wield a blade. He shattered armour with raw aetheric force, twisted the paths of fate, shielded his sons behind impossible barriers and reached into the minds of his enemies. Where lesser psykers risked being overwhelmed by the turbulence of the Warp, the presence of the Crimson King seemed instead to force that turbulence into obedience. Magnus the Red | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 6 | 4 | 10 | 1+ Force Organisation: • Lord of War Unit Composition: • 1 Magnus the Red Unit Type: • Infantry (Character) Wargear: • Horned Raiment • Blade of Ahn-Nunurta • Arcane Litanies Special Rules: • Primarch • Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) • Psyker (Mastery Level 4\) • Lord of the Ether • The Crimson King • The Warp Bends to Magnus • Sorcerous Duellist HORNED RAIMENT The war-plate of the Crimson King was as much an artefact of sorcery as of the artificer’s craft, surrounded by kine shields, illusions and shifting aetheric wards which distorted the form of Magnus and those who fought at his side. The Horned Raiment counts as Primarch Armour. When an enemy unit makes shooting attacks against Magnus or a unit he has joined, all To Hit rolls made as part of that attack suffer a -1 modifier. To Hit rolls made with Blast weapons suffer a -2 modifier instead. These modifiers have no effect in close combat. BLADE OF AHN-NUNURTA A weapon of ancient and esoteric provenance, the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta served as both a weapon of war and a focus through which Magnus could channel his immense psychic power. The Blade of Ahn-Nunurta is a Master-crafted, Two-Handed Force Weapon. It follows all normal ProHammer rules for Force Weapons. ARCANE LITANIES Inscribed upon the armour and wargear of the Crimson King were formulae, wards and occult geometries intended to protect their bearer against the dangers inherent in drawing upon the Warp. Once per battle, when Magnus suffers a Wound as a result of Perils of the Warp, he may ignore that Wound. The Psychic Test and psychic power are otherwise resolved normally. PSYKER — MASTERY LEVEL 4 Magnus the Red is a Psyker with Mastery Level 4. Before the battle, Magnus selects five psychic powers in total. These powers may be selected from the following disciplines: Biomancy, Divination, Pyromancy, Telekinesis and Telepathy. Magnus must select powers from at least two different psychic disciplines. He may not select all five powers from a single discipline. Infernal Phoenix and Strands of Fate are always known by Magnus and count towards his five psychic powers. Magnus otherwise follows all normal ProHammer rules for Psykers unless specifically stated otherwise. Magnus counts as belonging to all five Prosperine Cults for the purposes of army selection and other friendly abilities, but gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery from them. PSYCHIC SUPREMACY Magnus may never have more than two Blessings affecting himself at the same time. If Magnus successfully invokes a Blessing upon himself while already affected by two Blessings, he must immediately choose one existing Blessing affecting him to end before the new power takes effect. This restriction does not limit Blessings affecting friendly units or Maledictions affecting enemy units. Magnus may never have more than two psychic powers with ongoing effects simultaneously affecting himself and/or the opposing Primarch. LORD OF THE ETHER Magnus may invoke up to four psychic powers during each player turn, overriding the normal ProHammer maximum of two. Magnus may invoke multiple Witchfire powers during the same Shooting phase, subject to his normal limit of four psychic powers. Magnus may not successfully invoke the same psychic power more than once during the same player turn. Activating the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta as a Force Weapon does not count against this limit. THE CRIMSON KING The psychic mastery of Magnus was such that even a faltering connection to the Warp could be seized and brought under control through sheer force of will. Magnus may re-roll one failed Psychic Test during each player turn. The second result must be accepted. Magnus may attempt to Deny the Witch against any psychic power if either the Psyker invoking the power or its target is within 24" of Magnus. Resolve the Deny the Witch attempt normally using Magnus' own Mastery Level. Each psychic power may still only be the subject of one Deny the Witch attempt, as normal. THE WARP BENDS TO MAGNUS Where lesser psykers struggled as the immaterium became increasingly disturbed, Magnus imposed his will upon the Warp with a power few beings could hope to equal. Magnus ignores all Leadership penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp. In addition, psychic powers successfully invoked by Magnus are ignored when determining the penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp for other friendly Psykers. Psychic powers successfully invoked by other friendly Psykers still contribute towards Disturbance in the Warp normally. Magnus may also attempt to deny the witch at 18” around him. INFERNAL PHOENIX Witchfire — Beam Magnus unleashes a torrent of incandescent aetheric fire, burning through flesh and armour as it tears across the battlefield. Range 24" | Strength 8 | AP1 | Melta Resolve Infernal Phoenix using the normal rules for Beam psychic powers. STRANDS OF FATE Malediction Magnus reaches into the skeins of possibility and twists them about his victim, turning every intended action into another path towards failure. Choose one enemy non-Vehicle unit within 18" and line of sight. Until the beginning of Magnus’ next turn, that unit must pass a Leadership Test each time it attempts to: • Move during the Movement phase; • Shoot during the Shooting phase; or • declare a Charge. If the test is failed, the attempted action is lost and the unit may not attempt that type of action again during that phase. A separate Leadership Test is required for each different action attempted. SORCEROUS DUELLIST Even when locked in single combat with one of his brother Primarchs, Magnus remained first and foremost a master of the immaterium. While Magnus is fighting a Primarch Duel, at the beginning of each Assault phase he may invoke one of the following: • a Blessing which targets only Magnus; or • a Malediction which targets only the opposing Primarch. This psychic power counts towards Magnus’ maximum allowance of four powers for that player turn. All normal Psychic Tests, Deny the Witch attempts and Perils of the Warp rules apply. PRIMARCH RETINUE Magnus the Red may select any one of the following as his Primarch Retinue: • Legion Honour Guard Squad • Legion Terminator Command Squad • Sekhmet Terminator Cabal A retinue selected in this manner does not occupy an additional Force Organisation selection and otherwise follows the normal Primarch Retinue rules. THE CRIMSON KING’S GUARD A Legion Honour Guard Squad or Legion Terminator Command Squad selected as Magnus’ Primarch Retinue may be upgraded to a Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1\) for +25 points per squad. A squad upgraded in this manner is assigned to one Prosperine Cult and receives the normal Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits of that Cult. The squad selects one psychic power from any of the normal psychic disciplines available to Thousand Sons Psykers and otherwise follows all normal rules for a Psychic Brotherhood.
RULE id=r18-ts-magnus-psyker name=Psyker — Mastery Level 4 host=r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king hidden=false
Magnus knows five psychic powers in total. Infernal Phoenix and Strands of Fate are always known and count toward the five; the remaining three are selected from Biomancy, Divination, Pyromancy, Telekinesis and Telepathy, using at least two different disciplines. Magnus counts as belonging to all five Prosperine Cults for army selection/friendly abilities but gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery from them.
RULE id=r41-unit-xv-12-magnus-shard-of-the-crimson-king-source name=Source Entry host=r41-unit-xv-12-magnus-shard-of-the-crimson-king hidden=true
A Fragment of the Crimson King Magnus was shattered long before his final damnation was complete. Fragments of his soul were scattered throughout the Warp and across the material realm, each possessing only a portion of the Crimson King’s impossible power. Such a shard is less a creature than a psychic apparition — a thought given form, flickering between reality and unreality. Bullets pass through it, shells are cast aside by distortions in space and only weapons touched by the Warp can reliably harm it. Yet the manifestation is unstable, and the same psychic energies which empower it may at any moment tear it from reality entirely. Magnus, Shard of the Crimson King | 7 | 7 | 7 | \ | 7\ | 6 | 5 | 10 | – Force Organisation: • Lord of War Unit Composition: • 1 Magnus, Shard of the Crimson King Unit Type: • Monstrous Creature (Character) Wargear: • Aetheric Blade Special Rules: • Fear • Fearless • Master of the Legion • Psyker (Mastery Level 5\) • Ethereal • Incorporeal Will • The Crimson King Unbound • Beyond the Prosperine Cults • Nothing to Bless • Master of the Great Ocean • Beyond the Perils of the Warp • The Warp Breathes • The Crimson King Shattered RESTRICTIONS Magnus, Shard of the Crimson King may only be selected for a Thousand Sons army. An army may not include both Magnus, Shard of the Crimson King and Magnus in his mortal form. AETHERIC BLADE The Aetheric Blade is a Master-crafted Force Weapon. Magnus resolves attacks made with it at Strength 7. ETHEREAL Magnus is not truly a physical creature, but a fragment of psychic consciousness temporarily given form. Magnus possesses seven Wounds, but has no conventional Toughness characteristic and receives no Armour or Invulnerable Save. Shooting Non-psychic shooting attacks may only hit Magnus on an unmodified roll of 6. A successful hit may only wound Magnus on an unmodified roll of 6. Weapons which normally hit automatically still require a 6 to wound Magnus. Magnus receives no save against successful Wounds. Blast Weapons Non-psychic Blast weapons cannot directly strike Magnus. If Magnus would be beneath the template after scatter has been resolved, move the template directly away from Magnus by the minimum distance required for him to no longer be beneath it. Other models may still be hit normally. Close Combat Attack Type To Wound Normal close-combat weapon 6+ Power Weapon, Power Fist, Thunder Hammer or equivalent 5+ Force Weapon 2+ Magnus receives no save against successful Wounds. If a Force Weapon inflicts one or more Wounds upon Magnus, its wielder may attempt to activate the weapon normally. If successful, each Wound inflicted by that Force Weapon becomes a Massive Wound and inflicts D3 Wounds. Psychic Attacks Psychic shooting attacks and Wounds caused directly by psychic powers ignore the normal shooting restrictions of Ethereal. Whenever such an effect requires Magnus to possess a Toughness characteristic, treat him as Toughness 7 for that effect only. Magnus receives no save against resulting Wounds. Instant Death and Massive Wounds Magnus is immune to Instant Death. Massive Wounds affect Magnus normally only when caused by an activated Force Weapon or by a psychic effect which specifically inflicts Massive Wounds. Otherwise, an attack which would cause Instant Death or a Massive Wound simply inflicts one Wound. INCORPOREAL WILL Magnus may never declare a charge or make a Pursuit move. He may fight normally if charged and may Consolidate normally after combat. Magnus may never join another unit and no model may join him. THE CRIMSON KING UNBOUND Magnus is a Mastery Level 5 Psyker. Before deployment, choose six psychic powers for Magnus from any combination of the following disciplines: • Biomancy • Divination • Pyromancy • Telekinesis • Telepathy Magnus also always knows the following two unique powers: • Infernal Phoenix • Strands of Fate These two powers do not count towards his six selected powers. Magnus may attempt to invoke up to five psychic powers during each player turn, specifically overriding the normal ProHammer maximum for Psykers.Magnus may successfully manifest each individual psychic power no more than once during the same player turn. Magnus may invoke up to two Witchfire powers during the same Shooting phase. INFERNAL PHOENIX Type: Witchfire, Beam Range S AP Special 24" 8 1 Melta Resolve Infernal Phoenix using the normal rules for a Beam psychic power. STRANDS OF FATE Type: Malediction Range: 18" Choose one enemy non-Vehicle unit within range and line of sight. Until the beginning of Magnus’ next turn, the affected unit must pass a Leadership Test each time it wishes to: • Move • Shoot • Charge If the test is failed, that action is lost for that phase. BEYOND THE PROSPERINE CULTS Magnus is not assigned to a Prosperine Cult and gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery benefit. He counts as belonging to the Thousand Sons for army selection and rules which refer to friendly Thousand Sons models. NOTHING TO BLESS Magnus may never be the target of a Blessing, whether manifested by himself or another Psyker. Any Blessing manifested by Magnus must target another eligible friendly unit. MASTER OF THE GREAT OCEAN Magnus ignores all Leadership penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp. In addition, psychic powers successfully invoked by Magnus do not count when determining Disturbance in the Warp penalties for other Psykers. Magnus remains subject to all other rules concerning Psychic Tests and Perils of the Warp. Magnus may attempt to Deny the Witch against any psychic power if either the Psyker invoking the power or its target is within 24" of Magnus. Resolve the Deny the Witch attempt normally using Magnus' own Mastery Level. Each psychic power may still only be the subject of one Deny the Witch attempt, as normal. BEYOND THE PERILS OF THE WARP Whenever Magnus would suffer Perils of the Warp, ignore the normal Perils result. Magnus suffers no Wound or other effect. Instead, place one Instability Counter beside him. Each Instability Counter imposes a cumulative –1 modifier on Magnus’ next Warp Breath roll. After that Warp Breath roll has been resolved, remove all Instability Counters. There is no maximum number of Instability Counters. THE WARP BREATHES At the beginning of each Thousand Sons turn in which Magnus is on the battlefield, roll a D6 and apply any modifiers from Instability Counters. Modified Result Effect 1 or less The Crimson King Fades 2–5 Reality Holds 6+ The Warp Ascendant THE CRIMSON KING FADES Magnus immediately disappears from the battlefield and is placed in Reserve. He is not destroyed. Beginning on the following Thousand Sons turn, roll normally for Magnus to return from Reserve. When Magnus returns, he enters play using Deep Strike and scatters normally. Magnus may use psychic powers during the turn in which he returns. If Magnus remains in Reserve when the battle ends, he counts as destroyed for Victory Point purposes. This does not trigger The Crimson King Shattered. REALITY HOLDS No additional effect occurs. THE WARP ASCENDANT Until the beginning of the next Thousand Sons turn, every Psyker on the battlefield, friendly or enemy, may re-roll failed Psychic Tests. However, any Psychic Test containing a double causes Perils of the Warp, even if the Psychic Test succeeds. A double which is subsequently re-rolled still causes Perils. Magnus converts any Perils he suffers into Instability Counters as normal. THE CRIMSON KING SHATTERED If Magnus loses his final Wound and is actually destroyed, the psychic backlash tears through the minds of his sons. Immediately, every friendly non-Vehicle unit with Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) suffers: D3 Strength 4 AP– hits. Then every friendly Thousand Sons Psyker or Brotherhood of Psykers suffers Perils of the Warp. A Thousand Sons Independent Character with the Psyker rule suffers Perils twice instead, resolving each result separately. These effects ignore range and line of sight. For the purposes of Signs and Portents, all Perils caused by The Crimson King Shattered count as one single triggering event. Apply Signs and Portents once after all resulting Perils and casualties have been resolved. |
RULE id=r18-final-shard-cults name=Beyond the Prosperine Cults host=r41-unit-xv-12-magnus-shard-of-the-crimson-king hidden=false
Magnus is not assigned to a Prosperine Cult and gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery benefit. He counts as Thousand Sons for army selection and rules referring to friendly Thousand Sons models.
RULE id=r42-role-xv-crimson-magnus-hq-r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-source name=Source Entry host=r42-role-xv-crimson-magnus-hq-r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king hidden=false
Primarch of the Thousand Sons, the Crimson King, Lord of Prospero Magnus the Red was the most psychically gifted of the Emperor’s sons, a towering scholar-warrior whose understanding of the immaterium exceeded that of all but the greatest minds of the Imperium. Upon Prospero he gathered philosophers, occultists and psykers into a civilisation devoted to knowledge, while the Thousand Sons themselves became masters of disciplines that other Legions regarded with suspicion and fear. Upon the battlefield Magnus wielded sorcery as naturally as another warrior might wield a blade. He shattered armour with raw aetheric force, twisted the paths of fate, shielded his sons behind impossible barriers and reached into the minds of his enemies. Where lesser psykers risked being overwhelmed by the turbulence of the Warp, the presence of the Crimson King seemed instead to force that turbulence into obedience. Magnus the Red | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 6 | 4 | 10 | 1+ Force Organisation: • Lord of War Unit Composition: • 1 Magnus the Red Unit Type: • Infantry (Character) Wargear: • Horned Raiment • Blade of Ahn-Nunurta • Arcane Litanies Special Rules: • Primarch • Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) • Psyker (Mastery Level 4\) • Lord of the Ether • The Crimson King • The Warp Bends to Magnus • Sorcerous Duellist HORNED RAIMENT The war-plate of the Crimson King was as much an artefact of sorcery as of the artificer’s craft, surrounded by kine shields, illusions and shifting aetheric wards which distorted the form of Magnus and those who fought at his side. The Horned Raiment counts as Primarch Armour. When an enemy unit makes shooting attacks against Magnus or a unit he has joined, all To Hit rolls made as part of that attack suffer a -1 modifier. To Hit rolls made with Blast weapons suffer a -2 modifier instead. These modifiers have no effect in close combat. BLADE OF AHN-NUNURTA A weapon of ancient and esoteric provenance, the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta served as both a weapon of war and a focus through which Magnus could channel his immense psychic power. The Blade of Ahn-Nunurta is a Master-crafted, Two-Handed Force Weapon. It follows all normal ProHammer rules for Force Weapons. ARCANE LITANIES Inscribed upon the armour and wargear of the Crimson King were formulae, wards and occult geometries intended to protect their bearer against the dangers inherent in drawing upon the Warp. Once per battle, when Magnus suffers a Wound as a result of Perils of the Warp, he may ignore that Wound. The Psychic Test and psychic power are otherwise resolved normally. PSYKER — MASTERY LEVEL 4 Magnus the Red is a Psyker with Mastery Level 4. Before the battle, Magnus selects five psychic powers in total. These powers may be selected from the following disciplines: Biomancy, Divination, Pyromancy, Telekinesis and Telepathy. Magnus must select powers from at least two different psychic disciplines. He may not select all five powers from a single discipline. Infernal Phoenix and Strands of Fate are always known by Magnus and count towards his five psychic powers. Magnus otherwise follows all normal ProHammer rules for Psykers unless specifically stated otherwise. Magnus counts as belonging to all five Prosperine Cults for the purposes of army selection and other friendly abilities, but gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery from them. PSYCHIC SUPREMACY Magnus may never have more than two Blessings affecting himself at the same time. If Magnus successfully invokes a Blessing upon himself while already affected by two Blessings, he must immediately choose one existing Blessing affecting him to end before the new power takes effect. This restriction does not limit Blessings affecting friendly units or Maledictions affecting enemy units. Magnus may never have more than two psychic powers with ongoing effects simultaneously affecting himself and/or the opposing Primarch. LORD OF THE ETHER Magnus may invoke up to four psychic powers during each player turn, overriding the normal ProHammer maximum of two. Magnus may invoke multiple Witchfire powers during the same Shooting phase, subject to his normal limit of four psychic powers. Magnus may not successfully invoke the same psychic power more than once during the same player turn. Activating the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta as a Force Weapon does not count against this limit. THE CRIMSON KING The psychic mastery of Magnus was such that even a faltering connection to the Warp could be seized and brought under control through sheer force of will. Magnus may re-roll one failed Psychic Test during each player turn. The second result must be accepted. Magnus may attempt to Deny the Witch against any psychic power if either the Psyker invoking the power or its target is within 24" of Magnus. Resolve the Deny the Witch attempt normally using Magnus' own Mastery Level. Each psychic power may still only be the subject of one Deny the Witch attempt, as normal. THE WARP BENDS TO MAGNUS Where lesser psykers struggled as the immaterium became increasingly disturbed, Magnus imposed his will upon the Warp with a power few beings could hope to equal. Magnus ignores all Leadership penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp. In addition, psychic powers successfully invoked by Magnus are ignored when determining the penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp for other friendly Psykers. Psychic powers successfully invoked by other friendly Psykers still contribute towards Disturbance in the Warp normally. Magnus may also attempt to deny the witch at 18” around him. INFERNAL PHOENIX Witchfire — Beam Magnus unleashes a torrent of incandescent aetheric fire, burning through flesh and armour as it tears across the battlefield. Range 24" | Strength 8 | AP1 | Melta Resolve Infernal Phoenix using the normal rules for Beam psychic powers. STRANDS OF FATE Malediction Magnus reaches into the skeins of possibility and twists them about his victim, turning every intended action into another path towards failure. Choose one enemy non-Vehicle unit within 18" and line of sight. Until the beginning of Magnus’ next turn, that unit must pass a Leadership Test each time it attempts to: • Move during the Movement phase; • Shoot during the Shooting phase; or • declare a Charge. If the test is failed, the attempted action is lost and the unit may not attempt that type of action again during that phase. A separate Leadership Test is required for each different action attempted. SORCEROUS DUELLIST Even when locked in single combat with one of his brother Primarchs, Magnus remained first and foremost a master of the immaterium. While Magnus is fighting a Primarch Duel, at the beginning of each Assault phase he may invoke one of the following: • a Blessing which targets only Magnus; or • a Malediction which targets only the opposing Primarch. This psychic power counts towards Magnus’ maximum allowance of four powers for that player turn. All normal Psychic Tests, Deny the Witch attempts and Perils of the Warp rules apply. PRIMARCH RETINUE Magnus the Red may select any one of the following as his Primarch Retinue: • Legion Honour Guard Squad • Legion Terminator Command Squad • Sekhmet Terminator Cabal A retinue selected in this manner does not occupy an additional Force Organisation selection and otherwise follows the normal Primarch Retinue rules. THE CRIMSON KING’S GUARD A Legion Honour Guard Squad or Legion Terminator Command Squad selected as Magnus’ Primarch Retinue may be upgraded to a Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1\) for +25 points per squad. A squad upgraded in this manner is assigned to one Prosperine Cult and receives the normal Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits of that Cult. The squad selects one psychic power from any of the normal psychic disciplines available to Thousand Sons Psykers and otherwise follows all normal rules for a Psychic Brotherhood.

      <ns0:rules>
        <ns0:rule name="Source Entry" id="r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-source" hidden="true">
          <ns0:description>Primarch of the Thousand Sons, the Crimson King, Lord of Prospero
Magnus the Red was the most psychically gifted of the Emperor’s sons, a towering scholar-warrior whose understanding of the immaterium exceeded that of all but the greatest minds of the Imperium. Upon Prospero he gathered philosophers, occultists and psykers into a civilisation devoted to knowledge, while the Thousand Sons themselves became masters of disciplines that other Legions regarded with suspicion and fear.
Upon the battlefield Magnus wielded sorcery as naturally as another warrior might wield a blade. He shattered armour with raw aetheric force, twisted the paths of fate, shielded his sons behind impossible barriers and reached into the minds of his enemies. Where lesser psykers risked being overwhelmed by the turbulence of the Warp, the presence of the Crimson King seemed instead to force that turbulence into obedience.
Magnus the Red | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 7 | 6 | 4 | 10 | 1+
Force Organisation:
• Lord of War
Unit Composition:
• 1 Magnus the Red
Unit Type:
• Infantry (Character)
Wargear:
• Horned Raiment
• Blade of Ahn-Nunurta
• Arcane Litanies
Special Rules:
• Primarch
• Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons)
• Psyker (Mastery Level 4\)
• Lord of the Ether
• The Crimson King
• The Warp Bends to Magnus
• Sorcerous Duellist
HORNED RAIMENT The war-plate of the Crimson King was as much an artefact of sorcery as of the artificer’s craft, surrounded by kine shields, illusions and shifting aetheric wards which distorted the form of Magnus and those who fought at his side. The Horned Raiment counts as Primarch Armour. When an enemy unit makes shooting attacks against Magnus or a unit he has joined, all To Hit rolls made as part of that attack suffer a -1 modifier. To Hit rolls made with Blast weapons suffer a -2 modifier instead. These modifiers have no effect in close combat. BLADE OF AHN-NUNURTA A weapon of ancient and esoteric provenance, the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta served as both a weapon of war and a focus through which Magnus could channel his immense psychic power. The Blade of Ahn-Nunurta is a Master-crafted, Two-Handed Force Weapon. It follows all normal ProHammer rules for Force Weapons. ARCANE LITANIES Inscribed upon the armour and wargear of the Crimson King were formulae, wards and occult geometries intended to protect their bearer against the dangers inherent in drawing upon the Warp. Once per battle, when Magnus suffers a Wound as a result of Perils of the Warp, he may ignore that Wound. The Psychic Test and psychic power are otherwise resolved normally. PSYKER — MASTERY LEVEL 4 Magnus the Red is a Psyker with Mastery Level 4. Before the battle, Magnus selects five psychic powers in total. These powers may be selected from the following disciplines: Biomancy, Divination, Pyromancy, Telekinesis and Telepathy. Magnus must select powers from at least two different psychic disciplines. He may not select all five powers from a single discipline. Infernal Phoenix and Strands of Fate are always known by Magnus and count towards his five psychic powers. Magnus otherwise follows all normal ProHammer rules for Psykers unless specifically stated otherwise. Magnus counts as belonging to all five Prosperine Cults for the purposes of army selection and other friendly abilities, but gains no Cult Arcana or Cult Mastery from them. PSYCHIC SUPREMACY Magnus may never have more than two Blessings affecting himself at the same time. If Magnus successfully invokes a Blessing upon himself while already affected by two Blessings, he must immediately choose one existing Blessing affecting him to end before the new power takes effect. This restriction does not limit Blessings affecting friendly units or Maledictions affecting enemy units. Magnus may never have more than two psychic powers with ongoing effects simultaneously affecting himself and/or the opposing Primarch. LORD OF THE ETHER Magnus may invoke up to four psychic powers during each player turn, overriding the normal ProHammer maximum of two. Magnus may invoke multiple Witchfire powers during the same Shooting phase, subject to his normal limit of four psychic powers. Magnus may not successfully invoke the same psychic power more than once during the same player turn. Activating the Blade of Ahn-Nunurta as a Force Weapon does not count against this limit. THE CRIMSON KING The psychic mastery of Magnus was such that even a faltering connection to the Warp could be seized and brought under control through sheer force of will. Magnus may re-roll one failed Psychic Test during each player turn. The second result must be accepted. Magnus may attempt to Deny the Witch against any psychic power if either the Psyker invoking the power or its target is within 24" of Magnus. Resolve the Deny the Witch attempt normally using Magnus' own Mastery Level. Each psychic power may still only be the subject of one Deny the Witch attempt, as normal. THE WARP BENDS TO MAGNUS Where lesser psykers struggled as the immaterium became increasingly disturbed, Magnus imposed his will upon the Warp with a power few beings could hope to equal. Magnus ignores all Leadership penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp. In addition, psychic powers successfully invoked by Magnus are ignored when determining the penalties caused by Disturbance in the Warp for other friendly Psykers. Psychic powers successfully invoked by other friendly Psykers still contribute towards Disturbance in the Warp normally. Magnus may also attempt to deny the witch at 18” around him. INFERNAL PHOENIX Witchfire — Beam Magnus unleashes a torrent of incandescent aetheric fire, burning through flesh and armour as it tears across the battlefield. Range 24" | Strength 8 | AP1 | Melta Resolve Infernal Phoenix using the normal rules for Beam psychic powers. STRANDS OF FATE Malediction Magnus reaches into the skeins of possibility and twists them about his victim, turning every intended action into another path towards failure. Choose one enemy non-Vehicle unit within 18" and line of sight. Until the beginning of Magnus’ next turn, that unit must pass a Leadership Test each time it attempts to: • Move during the Movement phase; • Shoot during the Shooting phase; or • declare a Charge. If the test is failed, the attempted action is lost and the unit may not attempt that type of action again during that phase. A separate Leadership Test is required for each different action attempted. SORCEROUS DUELLIST Even when locked in single combat with one of his brother Primarchs, Magnus remained first and foremost a master of the immaterium. While Magnus is fighting a Primarch Duel, at the beginning of each Assault phase he may invoke one of the following: • a Blessing which targets only Magnus; or • a Malediction which targets only the opposing Primarch. This psychic power counts towards Magnus’ maximum allowance of four powers for that player turn. All normal Psychic Tests, Deny the Witch attempts and Perils of the Warp rules apply. PRIMARCH RETINUE Magnus the Red may select any one of the following as his Primarch Retinue: • Legion Honour Guard Squad • Legion Terminator Command Squad • Sekhmet Terminator Cabal A retinue selected in this manner does not occupy an additional Force Organisation selection and otherwise follows the normal Primarch Retinue rules. THE CRIMSON KING’S GUARD A Legion Honour Guard Squad or Legion Terminator Command Squad selected as Magnus’ Primarch Retinue may be upgraded to a Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1\) for +25 points per squad. A squad upgraded in this manner is assigned to one Prosperine Cult and receives the normal Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits of that Cult. The squad selects one psychic power from any of the normal psychic disciplines available to Thousand Sons Psykers and otherwise follows all normal rules for a Psychic Brotherhood.</ns0:description>
        </ns0:rule>

      <ns0:rules>
        <ns0:rule name="Source Entry" id="live-r2-r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-retinue-2-r41-unit-xv-0-sekhmet-terminator-cabal-source" hidden="true">
          <ns0:description>Sekhmet Terminator | 5 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 2 | 3 | 2 | 9 | 2+/4+
Sekhmet Inceptor | 5 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 2 | 3 | 3 | 10 | 2+/4+
SCARAB OCCULT The Sekhmet Terminator Cabal is a Psychic Brotherhood. When the army is selected, assign the Cabal to one Prosperine Cult normally. The Cabal receives both the Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits of its selected Prosperine Cult. The Cabal selects one psychic power from any of the normal ProHammer Psychic Disciplines available to Thousand Sons: • Biomancy • Divination • Pyromancy • Telekinesis • Telepathy Psychic Tests are taken using Leadership 9. While the Sekhmet Inceptor remains alive, the Cabal counts as Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 2), knows one additional psychic power from the disciplines above and takes Psychic Tests using Leadership 10. If the Inceptor is slain, the Cabal immediately returns to Mastery Level 1 and loses the second psychic power. PROSPERINE FORCE WEAPONS The Cabal uses the normal Prosperine Force Weapon rules from the Thousand Sons Armoury. A Psychic Brotherhood may attempt only one Force Weapon activation during each Assault phase. Activating the Cabal's Force Weapons counts as using one psychic power.
Force Organisation:
• Elites
• 0–1
Unit Composition:
• 5 Sekhmet Terminators
Unit Type:
• Infantry
• The Sekhmet Inceptor is a Character
Wargear:
• Cataphractii Terminator Armour
• Prosperine Force Weapon
• Foeblaster Boltgun
Special Rules:
• Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons)
• Fearless
• Brotherhood of Psykers (Mastery Level 1\)
• Scarab Occult
OPTIONS
• The Cabal may include up to five additional Sekhmet Terminators for +60 points per model.
• Any model may replace its Prosperine Force Weapon with:
• Power fist ........................................ Free
• Chainfist ................................... +10 points
• Any model may replace its Foeblaster Boltgun with a Combi-weapon at the normal Space Marine Armoury cost.
• For every five models in the Cabal, up to two Sekhmet may replace their Foeblaster Boltgun with:
• Heavy Flamer ............................... +10 points
• Plasma Blaster ............................. +15 points
• Reaper Autocannon ......................... +20 points
• One Sekhmet Terminator may be upgraded to:
• Sekhmet Inceptor ........................... +20 points
• The Sekhmet Inceptor may take:
• Grenade Harness ............................ +10 points
• The entire Cabal may take:
• Teleportation Transponders ................. +15 points
DEDICATED TRANSPORT
The Cabal may select a Land Raider, Dreadclaw Drop Pod or Spartan Assault Tank where Transport Capacity permits.</ns0:description>
        </ns0:rule>
      <ns0:rule id="r19-live-r2-r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-retinue-2-r41-unit-xv-0-sekhmet-terminator-cabal-scarab" name="Scarab Occult" hidden="false"><ns0:description>This unit is a Psychic Brotherhood. Assign it to one Prosperine Cult; it receives that Cult’s Arcana and Cult Mastery. It selects one power from the normal Thousand Sons disciplines and takes Psychic Tests on Leadership 9. While the Sekhmet Inceptor is alive, the Cabal is Mastery Level 2, knows one additional power and tests on Leadership 10; if he is slain it returns to Mastery Level 1 and loses the second power.</ns0:description></ns0:rule><ns0:rule id="r19-live-r2-r41-unit-xv-11-xv-magnus-the-red-the-crimson-king-retinue-2-r41-unit-xv-0-sekhmet-terminator-cabal-force" name="Prosperine Force Weapons" hidden="false"><ns0:description>The Cabal uses the normal Prosperine Force Weapon rules. It may attempt only one Force Weapon activation in each Assault phase, and that activation counts as using one psychic power.</ns0:description></ns0:rule></ns0:rules>
